[\[26a.3\]]khyod kyis dul ba legs pa dang /_yang dag par sdom pa legs pa dang /_don spyod pa legs pa dang /_chos spyod pa legs pa dang /_dge ba spyod pa legs pa dang /_dge legs spyod pa legs par rig nas skra dang kha spu bregs te/_gos ngur smrig bgos nas dad pa 
[\[26a.4\]]yang dag pa kho nas khyim nas khyim med par rab tu byung ba legs so/_/de nas byang chub sems dpa' shAkya thub pas kha lo sgyur ba la gsungs pa/_kha lo sgyur ba de'i phyir shing rta phyir sgyur cig_/pho brang nyid du 'dong ngo /_/'di ltar nga pho brang nyid du phyin nas don 'di kho na bsam 
[\[26a.5\]]par bya'o/_/kha lo sgyur bas shing rta phyir sgyur nas pho brang nyid du chas te/_der pho brang du gshegs pa dang /_byang chub sems dpa' shAkya thub pa kye ma'o bdag ni skra dang kha spu bregs te gos ngur smrig bgos nas dad pa yang dag pa kho nas khyim nas khyim med par rab tu byung 
[\[26a.6\]]bar bya'o snyam pa'i don de nyid dgongs so/_/de nas rgyal po zas gtsang gis kha lo sgyur ba la smras pa la sogs pa ni snga ma bzhin no/_/de nas skye dgu'i bdag mo chen mos rmi lam rmis pa dbang po'i tog lhung ba dang /_shAkya rnams phyag dar gyis khengs pa dang /_sa ldog 
[\[26a.7\]]pa dang /_dpal khyim nas byung ba dang /_phyi rol na skad cig cing ngu zhing dgod pa la bdag nyid lta ba dang /_nyi ma gang nas shar ba de nyid nas nub pa'o/_/byang chub sems dpas de gsan pa dang /_dgongs pa nga ni shAkya rnams kyi dbang po'i tog gi gnas yin pa dang /_
[\[26b\]]
[\[26b.1\]]nga grong khyer nas 'byung na dpal 'byung bar 'gyur ro/_/rgyal po zas gtsang mas kyang rmi lam rmis pa dpal gyi mal khri chag pa dang /_pha'i rgyud chad pa'o/_/byang chub sems dpas de gsan pa dang /_de de bzhin te/_nga ni grags 'dzin ma'i dpal gyi gnas su 
[\[26b.2\]]gyur pa dang /_zas gtsang ma'i pha'i rigs rgyud kyi gnas su gyur pas nga rab tu byung na shAkya rnams kyi rigs rgyud chad par 'gyur ro/_/mtshan mkhan rnams kyis rgyal po zas gtsang ma'i mdun du gal te gzhon nu zhag bdun gyi bar du rab tu ma byung na 'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal 
[\[26b.3\]]por 'gyur ro zhes lung bstan to/_/'di skad ces kyang smras so/_/gal te de yi bzang po gzigs bzhed na/_/de slad dgung bdun bar du srungs shig dang /_/mi dbang gdugs ni bdal bar gyur pa lta'i/_/rin chen sna bdun 'byung bar the tsom med/_/khyod sras 
[\[26b.4\]]rin chen bdun po yi/_/dbang phyug gi ni rgyal por 'gyur/_/rgya mtsho la ni thug pa'i sa/_/chad pa med pas skyong bar 'gyur/_/'on te sa ni spangs nas su/_/bsnyengs med nags su bzhud na ni/_/thams cad mkhyen pa thob gyur nas/_/'gro ba sgrol ba mdzad 
[\[26b.5\]]par 'gyur/_/zhes thos so/_/thos nas kyang zas gtsang dang /_zas dkar dang /_bre bo zas dang /_bdud rtsi zas bzhi lhan cig gcig tu tshogs nas gros bya bar brtsams pa/_shes ldan dag mtshan mkhan dang ltas mkhan gyi bram ze rnams kyis byang chub sems dpa'

[\[26b.7\]]de rnams kyis ser skya'i gzhi'i grong khyer ra ba rim pa bdun dang /_'obs rim pa bdun gyis bskor nas grong khyer gyi sgo 'phar lcags las byas shing sgo 'phar rnams la dril bu shin tu sgra gsal ba btags te/_sgo phye na de'i sgras dpag tshad gcig khor yug tu grag par 'gyur ro/_/
[\[27a\]]
[\[27a.1\]]byang chub sems dpa' khang [{]bzangs kyi [,]bzang gi [}]gzhi na bud med yid 'phrog pa/_gzugs bzang ba rnams dang gar dang /_glu dang /_rol mo rnams kyi spyod pas bsnyen bkur byed du bcug go/_/skas shin tu che ba spor rung ba mi lnga brgyas bteg pa byas so/_/
[\[27a.2\]]chibs bsngags ldan gyi sa phyogs ni zangs ma'i glegs bus g.yogs shing gser gyi sil dkrol gyi dra ba btags pas bskor to/_/byang chub sems dpa' ni steng gi khang [{]bzangs kyi [,]bzang gi [}]gzhi skyes pa med par zhugs nas/_pi bang dang /_gling bu dang /_khar rnga dang /_sgra 
[\[27a.3\]]snyan la sogs pa rol mo'i cha byad dag nye bar len cing 'byor pa chen pos bzhugs so/_/mtshan mo ni rgyal po zas gtsang yang dpung gi tshogs yan lag bzhi dang shar sgo'i phyi rol na 'dug go/_/lho phyogs na ni bre bo zas so/_/nub phyogs na ni zas dkar ro/_/byang phyogs 
[\[27a.4\]]na ni bdud rtsi zas so/_/grong khyer gyi dbus kyi 'dram po byed pa ni shAkya ming chen dpung gi tshogs yan lag bzhi dang ldan pa'o/_/de shar sgor song nas smras pa/_su zhig mel tse byed/_su zhig mel tse byed/_rgyal po zas gtsang gis smras pa/_kho bo mel tse byed 
[\[27a.5\]]do/_/lha mel tse mdzod cig_/mel tse mdzad dang khyod la legs par 'gyur zhes smras te tshigs su bcad de smras pa/_'jig rten gnyid log ro dang mtshungs/_/gnyid log bdud kyi dbang du song /_/'di ni mi nyal rtag tu mkhas/_/de bas ma nyal mel tse byos/_/lho 
[\[27a.6\]]sgor song nas smras pa/_su zhig mel tse byed/_su zhig mel tse byed/_bre bo zas kyis smras pa/_kho bo mel tse byed do/_/lha mel tse mdzod cig_/mel tse mdzad dang /_khyod la legs par 'gyur ro zhes smras nas tshigs su bcad de smras pa/_'jig rten gnyid

[\[27b.5\]]nangs nas shAkya ming chen gyis rgyal po zas gtsang la smras pa/_lha dgung sangs lags te/_dgung bdun la dgung gcig ni thal dgung drug ni lus so/_/rgyal po zas gtsang gis smras pa/_legs kyis zhag drug 'das nas gzhon nu 'khor los sgyur bar gyur na bdag 
[\[27b.6\]]cag nam mkha'i ngos la rgyu zhing gling bzhir phyi bzhin 'dong ngo /_/de bzhin du dgung gcig lus so zhes bya ba'i bar du'o/_/de nas lha'i dbang po brgya byin 'di snyam du sems te/_mi dbang shAkya'i sras po thub pa 'di/_/bdag nyid chen po pha rol phyin drug rdzogs/_/thugs 
[\[27b.7\]]rnams dgyes par mdzad pa dka' thub tshal/_/'phags pa dgyes pa'i nags su gshegs par bzhed/_/de nas byang chub sems dpa' 'di snyam du dgongs te/_bdag la gzhan dag gzhon nu shAkya thub pa ni skyes pa ma yin te/_gang gi phyir grags 'dzin ma dang /_
[\[28a\]]
[\[28a.1\]]sa 'tsho ma dang /[ {]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]skyes dang /_bud med drug khri spangs nas rab tu 'byung ngo zhes gzhi de las kha zer bar 'gyur du 'ong gis ma la bdag gis grags 'dzin ma dang lhan cig dga' mgur spyad par bya'o snyam nas des grags 'dzin ma dang lhan cig dga' mgur spyad 
[\[28a.2\]]pa dang grags 'dzin ma sems can dang ldan par gyur nas/_de 'di snyam du sems te/_nam nangs nas byang chub sems dpa'i snyan du gsol lo snyam mo/_/de nas byang chub sems dpa' de'i tshe rten cing 'brel par 'byung ba dang ldan pas tshigs su bcad de gsungs pa/_bud med dang 
[\[28a.3\]]bdag khyim nyal ba'i/_/tha ma deng gi 'di yin te/_/phyin cad khyim du bud med dang /_/nga ni lhan cig mi gnas so/_/de nas bud med de rnams gar dang /_glu dang /_rol mos ngal zhing lci la dub nas kha nas kha chu 'dzag cing skra zhig la gos dang lag pa dag lhug par btang nas bla 
[\[28a.4\]]ba'i tshig byung rgyal du rdol zhing gnyid log go/_/byang chub sems dpas bud med de dag thams cad kha nas kha chu 'dzag cing skra zhig la gos dang lag pa dag lhug par btang nas/_bla ba'i tshig byung rgyal du rdol zhing gnyid log pa gzigs so/_/gzigs nas kyang btsun mo'i 
[\[28a.5\]]'khor la dur khrod kyi 'du shes skyes so/_/de nas byang chub sems dpa' skyo ba 'khrungs pa dang tshigs su bcad de gsungs pa/_rlung gi shugs kyis pad ma'i tshal ltar sa gzhi 'dir/_/lag pa dag dang rkang pa brkyang nas 'gyel 'gyur te/_/skra rnams zhig cing 
[\[28a.6\]]mdoms dang rkub kyi phyogs snang ba/_/shAkya'i sras kyis gzigs nas rang bzhin skyo bar 'gyur/_/tshul dang bral ba'i bud med na chung tshogs 'di dag_/dur khrod 'jigs su rung ba'i rang bzhin 'drar mthong nas/_/sems can byis pa'i spyod yul la ni deng dga' na/_/sems 
[\[28a.7\]]dang blo gros dran dang bral ba yin nam ci/_/'dod pa tha chad 'dam dang mda' mdung dug me 'dra/_/rmi lam lta bu yin te tshwa chu'i ro dang mtshungs/_/'dod pa phung byed rtsod dang sdig pa'i rgyur gyur pa/_/mtha' ngan sbrul gyi kha 'dra bdag gis spang bar bya/_/de nas byang chub sems 
[\[28b\]]
[\[28b.1\]]dpa' gzims mal chen po la gzims pa na/_mtshan mo de nyid la rmi lam lnga rmis pas chen po nyid gzims mal du byas shing ri rab ri'i rgyal po dbu rten du bcug_/phyag g.yon pa shar phyogs kyi rgya mtsho chen por 'jug_/phyag g.yas pa nub phyogs kyi rgya mtsho chen por 'jug_/
[\[28b.2\]]zhabs gnyis rgya mtsho chen por 'jug pa dang /_'greng ba zhes bya ba'i rtsa'i rigs lte ba nas byung nas nam mkha'i bar du 'phags te 'dug pa dang /_bya kun nas dkar la mgo nag pa rnams kyis zhabs gnyis la gtugs nas pus mo'i lha nga la thug pa'i bar du lta ba dang /_bya sna tshogs 
[\[28b.3\]]phyogs bzhi nas 'ongs nas mdun du kha dog gcig tu 'dug pa dang /_mi gtsang ba'i ro'i steng nas bdag nyid mi gtsang bas ma gos par 'chag pa'o/_/de lta bu rmis nas kyang thugs dgyes te dgongs pa nga'i rmi lam 'di 'dra ba rmis pas ring po mi thogs pa kho nar bla na med pa'i 
[\[28b.4\]]ye shes thob par 'gyur ro snyam mo/_/grags 'dzin mas kyang rmi lam du mthong ba bdag gi so kun lhags pa dang /_dung gi dpung pa chag pa dang /_zla ba sa la lhung ba dang /_skar ma rnams ni ngu ba'o/_/grags 'dzin mas kyang rmi lam rmis pa de byang chub sems 
[\[28b.5\]]dpa' la gsol pa dang /_byang chub sems dpas gsungs pa/_khyod kyi so ni kha nang gnas/_/zla ba 'di ni mkhar gnas kyis/_/yid kyi gdung ba bsal nas ni/_/dogs med bde bar gnyid log cig_/de nas grags 'dzin ma 'di snyam du sems te/_rmi lam 
[\[28b.6\]]'jigs pa rmis pa na/_/bdag yid dogs dang bcas par 'gyur/_/deng gi mtshan mo sdug pa dang /_/'bral bdag tu mi 'gyur grang /_/de nas lha'i dbang po brgya byin 'di snyam du sems te/_byang chub sems dpa' rnams ni ji ltar rgyu'i stobs can yin mod kyi 'on 
[\[28b.7\]]kyang rkyen gyis bsgrub par bya dgos te/_'di ltar rgyu'i stobs chen po yang rkyen gyi stobs la ltos so snyam nas byang chub sems dpa' gang na ba der song ste phyin pa dang /_khang [{]bzangs kyi [,]bzang gi [}]gzhi la 'dug ste byang chub sems dpa' la 'di skad ces gsol to/_/nags kyi spre'u 
[\[29a\]]
[\[29a.1\]]ni spyod yul rgyu 'dra zhing /_/yan par 'dug pa'i rte'u dang 'dra ba'i thugs/_/gal te 'dod pa'i yon tan las bzlog na/_/de las shin tu gsal ba'i snang ba gzigs/_/blo gros bzang po bzhengs shig bzhengs/_/sa ni spangs te bzhud mdzod 
[\[29a.2\]]la/_/thams cad mkhyen nyid brnyes nas ni/_/'gro ba dag ni bsgral bar mdzod/_/byang chub sems dpas gsungs pa ni/_/kau shi ka ma mthong ngam/_/nga ni [{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]bdag po rgyas bskor ltar/_/mi ni mthu ldan gzhu mtshon ral gri thogs/_/rta glang shing rta dpung tshogs 
[\[29a.3\]]mang po yis/_/grong khyer ser skya kun nas yongs su bskor/_/zas gtsang la sogs shAkya'i tshogs 'di dag_/glang chen rta zhon legs par go bgos lus/_/mi sdug sna tshogs lag na mtshon thogs te/_/khyams dang rta babs khang [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]gzhi nang gnas/_/
[\[29a.4\]]pho brang dang ni khyim sgo dang /_/de bzhin grong khyer sgo dag kyang /_/bcad nas gtan pa dam bcug ste/_/de bzhin kun nas bsrungs par gyur/_/ru dung pa Ta ha sgra rnga sgra yis/_/khyams dang rta babs khang [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]nang gnas shing /_/glang chen rta dmag 'bad pa'i skye bo'i 
[\[29a.5\]]tshogs/_/rgyun du nga mi gtang phyir srung bar byed/_/brgya byin gyis gsol pa/_des pa mar me mdzad kyis lung bstan dang /_/sdug bsngal gyis gdungs 'jig rten 'di dang yang /_/khyod kyi sngon gyi dam bcas dgongs mdzod la/_/khab dag spangs te nges par 'byung bar 
[\[29a.6\]]mdzod/_/ji ltar khyod ni bgegs med par/_/deng nyid nags su gshegs 'gyur ba/_/bdag cag tshangs sogs bcas pa yi/_/lha rnams kyis ni de bzhin bgyi/_/byang chub sems dpas legs so zhes gsungs nas thugs dgyes par gyur to/_/de nas lha rnams kyi dbang 
[\[29a.7\]]po brgya byin gyis gnyid kyis myos par byas nas gnod sbyin gyi sde dpon chen po lngas rtsen la bsgo ba/_grogs po byang chub sems dpa' steng gi khang [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]nas babs kyis skas long shig_/des skas blangs pa la byang chub sems dpa' babs pa dang de nas brgya byin gyis brda byas 
[\[29b\]]
[\[29b.1\]]ma thag tu zhabs 'bring ba 'dun pa gang na ba der gshegs na ji tsam na 'dun pa gnyid stug pos non par gzigs nas byang chub sems dpas bsgrims te bslang nas tshigs su bcad pa'i dbyangs kyis gsungs pa/_'dun pa longs kye khyim nas myur bar ni/_/nga yi rta pho rin chen bsngags 
[\[29b.2\]]ldan long /_/sngon gyi rgyal bas longs spyad dka' thub tshal/_/thub rnams dgyes pa mdzad par nga gshegs so/_/'dun pa gnyid log pa'i mig phye nas smras pa/_lha khyod skyed mos tshal gyi dus ma bab/_/des pa khyod dang 'ga' yang 'khon ma mchis/_/khyod la dgra bo su yang ma mchis 
[\[29b.3\]]na/_/dgung phyed khyod la bcibs pa ci zhig bgyi/_/byang chub sems dpas bka' stsal pa/_'dun pa khyod kyis lan 'ga' yang /_/nga yi tshig ni ma bcag na/_/nga yi deng dus tha ma la/_/nga yi tshig 'di ma gcag shig_/'dun pas gsol pa/_gzhon nu mtshan mo 'di ni 'jigs pa dang 
[\[29b.4\]]'jigs su rung ba mchis pas bdag bcibs pa mi len no/_/byang chub sems dpas dgongs pa/_lan dang yang lan gyis gdon mi za bar skye bos da par 'gyur gyis bdag nyid song la rta'i rgyal po bsngags ldan sta gon bya'o snyam nas de nas nyid kyis pho brang gi sgo phye nas

[\[30a\]]
[\[30a.1\]]gshegs par chas pa mthong ngo /_/de nas skad 'dzer cing mig mchi mas gang bas smras pa/_gzhon nu 'di ci mdzad/_ming chen 'gro'o gzhon nu 'di ni rigs pa ma lags so/_/byang chub sems dpas gsungs pa/_ming chen 'di nyid rigs pa yin te/_

[\[30a.4\]]dang /_ming chen gdong las mchi ma char bzhin du 'bab cing cho nge 'debs pa la zhugs te kyi hud/_deng du rgyal po zas gtsang dang /_/shAkya dag ni thams cad kyis/_/yang dang yang du bsam pa yi/_/re ba 'bras bu med par gyur/_/deng du rgyal po zas gtsang ni/_/sras kyi 
[\[30a.5\]]mya ngan gyis gdungs pa/_/lag bsgreng bzhin du sdug bsngal zhing /_/mi bzad sgra ni 'byin par 'gyur/_/sa 'tsho grags 'dzin [{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]skyes/_/de bzhin btsun mo'i 'khor rgyas pa'ang /_/don grub dang ni bral ba yis/_/shin tu sdug bsngal myong bar 'gyur/_/zhes

[\[30b.1\]]'ga' yang sad par ma gyur to/_/de nas byang chub sems dpa' 'di snyam du dgongs te/_gang bdag gis yab la ma bltas par 'gro ba ni nga'i tshul dang 'thun pa ma yin no snyam nas rgyal po zas gtsang ma gang na ba der gshegs te byon pa dang /_rgyal po zas gtsang ma gnyid

[\[30b.5\]]chags yid kyis ni/_/'khor ba las ni rnam grol phyir/_/brtser ldan nye du spangs nas ni/_/skye bo med nags bstan pa lags/_/lus can 'du ba nges par ni/_/mthar ni 'bral 'gyur nyid lags pas/_/bdag ni rjes su dgongs nas ni/_/rmongs pas thugs ngan ma mdzad cig_/

[\[31a\]]
[\[31a.1\]]lags so/_/khyed rnams la stobs ci tsam yod/_myu gus smras pa/_bdag gis ni sa la ri ji snyed yod pa de phrag pa gcig tu thogs te nyam nga ba chung ngus 'gro nus pa de tsam gyi stobs yod do/_/mang ku ras smras pa/_bdag gis kyang sa la chu ji snyed

[\[31a.6\]]dpa' rnams la stobs 'di 'dra ba mnga' bar ma 'tshal to/_/de nas byang chub sems dpa' chibs rin po che bsngags ldan la bcibs te 'di ltar byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi byang chub sems dpa'i mthu dang lha rnams kyi lha'i mthus chibs rin po che bsngags ldan dang 'dun 
[\[31a.7\]]pa steng gi nam mkha' la 'phags so/_/de nas brgya byin dang tshangs pa la sogs pa lha rnams kyang lha brgya stong phrag du mas yongs su bskor nas byang chub sems dpa' gang na ba der song ste phyin pa dang /_byang chub sems dpa' bskor cing dong ste g.yas phyogs nas gzugs na 
[\[31b\]]
[\[31b.1\]]spyod pa'i lha rnams kyang spyod lam zhi ba dang /_g.yon phyogs nas 'dod pa na spyod pa rnams kyis rgyal mtshan dang ba dan bsgreng nas lha'i rol mo'i khyad par sna tshogs dang /_mdun nas brgya byin dang tshangs pa la sogs pa rang rang gi spyod lam gyis dong ngo /_/nam mkha'i ngos la

[\[32a\]]
[\[32a.1\]]byung nas 'di slar yang rang gi grong khyer du 'jug par mi 'gyur ram snyam pa la sku thams cad kyi g.yas phyogs su glang po che'i lta stangs kyis gzigs nas/_sangs rgyas rnams kyis bstan pa yi/_/lam gyi mchog ni ma thob par/_/grong khyer ser

[\[32a.3\]]pa/_'dun pa rgyan dang rta khyer la slar song shig_/'dir smras pa/_kha lo sgyur khyod nga yi rta 'di dang /_/rgyan 'di rnams kyang gnyen pa dag la byin/_/nga ni 'dod pa rgya chen spangs nas su/_/da lta 'di nyid du ni rab 'byung bya/_/'dod pa 'di ni spu gri'i so 'dra zhing /_/me 
[\[32a.4\]]'obs 'bar dang mtshungs shing mtha' ngan pa/_/'jig rten phan phyir snying rjes nga spangs te/_/dgon par song nas khyim du phyir mi ldog_/de nas 'dun pa gdong la mchi ma char bzhin 'bab pas byang chub sems dpa' la gsol pa/_seng ge stag gis gtams pa dang /_

[\[32a.6\]]pus sdug bsngal myong 'gyur gyi/_/'khor ba la ni grogs yod ma yin no/_/'dun pas gsol pa/_kyi hud gzhon nus sdug bsngal ma gzigs te/_/zhabs 'jam glang chen rta la chibs goms pa/_/sa gzhi rtswa dang rdo bas gtams rtsub par/_/ji ltar zhabs kyis

[\[32b.3\]]gyis blangs nas bkur sti chen po byas te sum cu rtsa gsum gyi lha rnams kyi nang du khyer nas gtsug phud kyi mchod pa chen po btsugs so/_/de nas lha'i dbang po brgya byin 'di snyam du sems te gzhon nu ni rgyal rigs kyi mngon pa'i nga rgyal gyis 'di bzlog par mi nus so snyam 
[\[32b.4\]]nas dga' bar gyur to/_/de nas byang chub sems dpas 'dun pa la gsungs pa/_khyod kyis bsngags ldan pho brang gi nang du ma bkri shig_/de skad ces gsungs pa dang /_'dun pa skad 'dzer cing mig mchi mas gang nas rta'i rgyal po bsngags ldan khrid de yang dang 
[\[32b.5\]]yang du byang chub sems dpa' la lta zhing song ste ser skya'i gzhir phyin pa dang der ca co skyes te ji tsam na rgyal po zas gtsang ma la smras pa/_lha gzhon nu byon gyis dgyes pa bskyed du gsol/_grong khyer ser skya'i gzhir yang sgra gsang chen po bsgrags so/_/rta'i rgyal po

[\[33b\]]
[\[33b.1\]]'gyur te/_de bas na bdag cag rnams sangs rgyas kyi zhing du gnas par mi nus pas na bdag cag yongs su mya ngan las 'da'o/_/sha na'i gos 'di rgyal po zas gtsang ma'i sras la khyed kyis phul cig_/bdag nyid chen po de ni bla na med pa'i ye shes thugs su chud par

[\[34a\]]
[\[34a.1\]]byin cig_/de nas lha rnams kyi dbang po brgya byin gyis byang chub sems dpa'i zhabs gnyis la gtugs nas sha na'i gos dag phul te ka shi ka'i ras phran rnams blangs so/_/byang chub sems dpa'i sku dang sha na'i gos dag 'tsham par ma gyur pa dang /_de 'di snyam 
[\[34a.2\]]du dgongs te kye ma'o sha na'i gos rnams bdag gi sku dang 'tsham par gyur cig snyam nas tshig smras ma thag tu sha na'i gos de dag 'di ltar byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi byang chub sems dpa'i mthu dang /_lha rnams kyi lha'i mthus byang chub sems dpa'i sku dang 'tsham par gyur to/_/de nas 
[\[34a.3\]]byang chub sems dpa' 'di snyam du dgongs te/_da ni bdag rab tu byung zin gyis 'gro ba rnams rjes su gzung bar bya'o snyam mo/_/de nas byang chub sems dpa' mthar gyis rgyu zhing gshegs pa dang rgyal po'i khab tu byon to/_/byang chub sems dpa' bzo'i gnas dang /_las 
[\[34a.4\]]kyi gnas de dang de rnams la mkhas pa yin pas des shing kar bI ra'i lo ma las lhung bzed byas nas spyod lam zhi bas rgyal po'i khab tu bsod snyoms kyi phyir zhugs so/_/de'i tshe rgyal po gzugs can snying po steng gi khang [{]bzangs kyi [,]bzang gi [}]gzhi na 'dug pa des 'gro ba dang /_ldog pa 
[\[34a.5\]]dang /_lta ba dang /_rnam par lta ba dang /_bskum pa dang /_brkyang ba dang /_chos gos snam sbyar dang /_lhung bzed thogs pa mdzes pas bsod snyoms spyod pa mthong ngo /_/mthong nas kyang 'di snyam du sems te/_rgyal po'i khab na rab tu byung ba ji snyed cig gnas pa 
[\[34a.6\]]la rab tu byung ba 'di'i spyod lam de lta bu ni 'ga' yang med do snyam mo/_/'dir smras pa/_ji ltar mig can 'gro ba ltar/_/rab tu byung ba bsgrags par byas/_/'dis ni brtag par mdzad nas su/_/rab tu byung ba yang dag bzhed/_/khyim gnas 'di ni sdug

[\[34b.1\](]ged+hA [,]ga d+hA [)]yi ni/_/grong mchog rgyal po'i khab gshegs nas/_/mgon po shes bzhin dran ldan pa/_/bsod snyoms phyir ni gshegs pa der/_/bzo sbyangs ma ga d+hA yi bdag_/khang [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]'di na 'dug pas mthong /_/mthong nas sems dang de nas ni/_/blon po rnams la 
[\[34b.2\]]'di skad smras/_/mtshan mchog gis ni rgyas gyur cing /_/kho lag phun sum tshogs pa dang /_/gnya' shing gang du lta ba yi/_/'di la shes ldan dag ltos shig_/spyan mi 'deg la yid gzhungs shing /_/dman pa'i rigs las ma 'khrungs 'di/_/gang du gnas par

[\[34b.4\]]nas ni/_/shes bzhin dran dang ldan pa yis/_/lhung bzed myur du gang bar mdzad/_/bsod snyoms spyad pa byas nas kyang /_/thub pa grong khyer phyir byung nas/_/skya bseng la ni mngon gshegs te/_/de la gnas ni btab par gyur/_/gnas btab pa ni shes nas 
[\[34b.5\]]kyang /_/pho nya gcig gis bsdad byas nas/_/gzhan ni myur du slar log ste/_/rgyal po la ni tshig smras pa/_/rgyal po chen po dge slong de/_/seng ge'am stag gi phru gu dag_/ri sul dag na gnas pa ltar/_/skya bseng gi ni steng na gnas/_/pho nya rnams kyi tshig thos

[\[35a\]]
[\[35a.1\]]spyod par mdzes ma lags/_/khang pa nyams dga' dag dang ni/_/bud med rgyan gyis brgyan pa dang /_/longs spyod mchog ni khyod gsol na/_/nyid kyi gdung ni zhu yis gsungs/_/rgyal po gangs ri nye ba na/_/yul gyi ljongs ni nor dag dang /_/'bru dag

[\[35a.5\]]yi blo ni thar pa la rten pa/_/'dod pa'i shugs kyis bskyod par mi nus so/_/'jig rten 'di ni 'dod par bying gyur nas/_/'khor ba'i 'khor los yongs su 'khyam pa la/_/mi dbang nga skyabs yin te gzhan med pas/_/'jigs pa che las de ni thar bar bya/_/'dod pa rnams 
[\[35a.6\]]kyi nyes dmigs mkhyen/_/mya ngan 'das pa zhi ba gzigs/_/spang phyir gang du nga yi yid/_/dga' bdag tu 'gro bar bya/_/rgyal po gzugs can snying pos gsol pa/_kye rab tu byung ba brtul zhugs 'dis ci zhig don du gnyer/_des gsungs pa/_bla na med pa

[\[35b.1\]]bzhin du bsam par bya'o zhes gsungs nas rgyal po'i khab nas byung ste bya rgod kyi phung po'i ri dang ha cang yang mi ring bar drang srong rnams kyi bsti gnas der gshegs so/_/de nas spyod pa dang gnas pas bsam gtan la brtson par gnas te/_de rnams gang gi tshe rkang pa gcig

[\[35b.5\]]nus pa ma yin/_/bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub par nus pa ma yin no/_/de nas byang chub sems dpas lam de nyid nus pa med par mkhyen nas/_sgyu rtsal shes kyi bu ring 'phur gang na ba der gshegs te byon pa dang /_sgyu rtsal shes kyi bu ring 'phur

[\[36a\]]
[\[36a.1\]]shes kyi bu ring 'phur la brtson 'grus dang dran pa dang /_ting nge 'dzin dang shes rab yod pa ltar bdag la yang brtson 'grus dang dran pa dang /_ting nge 'dzin dang /_shes rab yod pas sgyu rtsal shes kyi bu ring 'phur gyis ci yang med pa'i skye mched kyi bar gyi chos

[\[36b.1\]]mgu bar gyur to/_de nas byang chub sems dpa' 'di snyam du dgongs te/_lam 'di ni shes par nus pa ma yin/_mthong bar nus pa ma yin/_bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs par byang chub par nus pa ma yin no snyam mo/_/de nas byang chub sems dpas lam de nus pa

[\[37a\]]
[\[37a.1\]]mngon sum du byas sam/_tshe dang ldan pa gau ta ma mad lags te/_bdag gis 'du shes med 'du shes med min skye mched kyi bar gyi chos 'di dag mngon sum du bgyis so/_/byang chub sems dpas gsungs pa/_tshe dang ldan pa lhag spyod bdag gis

[\[37a.6\]]po dang lhan cig gang gA'i lho phyogs na lteng rgyas 'od srung gi sde'i grong rdal gang na ba der rgyu zhing gshegs te byon nas de phan tshun 'chag pa na phyogs dga' dga' ltar 'dug pa/_nags tshang tshing mdzes pa/_chu klung nai rany+dza nA bsil bar 'bab pa/_gse gu can 'jug 
[\[37a.7\]]ngogs bde zhing mtha' rgya che la gsing ma sngon po dang /_shing sna tshogs kyis brgyan pa dga' dga' ltar 'dug pa zhig gzigs so/_/gzigs nas kyang 'di snyam du dgongs te/_kye ma'o sa phyogs 'di ni dga' dga' ltar 'dug pa nags tshang tshing mdzes pa/_chu 
[\[37b\]]

[\[37b.5\]]thugs mngon du bzung /_mngon du bsdams/_shin tu mngon du bsdams pa la sogs pa ni snga ma bzhin no/_/de nas byang chub sems dpa' 'di snyam du dgongs te/_ma la bdag gis rnam par bam pa'i bsam gtan dag la snyoms par 'jug par bya'o snyam mo/_/des

[\[38a\]]
[\[38a.1\]]mi bzad pa skye ba de bzhin du/_byang chub sems dpa' rnam par bam pa'i bsam gtan dag la snyoms par zhugs pa na/_dbu la dbu'i tshor ba shin tu mi bzad pa skyes kyang de zhum pa ma yin gyi/_brtson 'grus ba rtsams pa yin/_sku ma sbyangs pa ma yin

[\[38a.3\]]pa'i phyir thugs yongs su gtugs te mi gnas so/_/de 'di snyam du dgongs te/_ma la bdag gis lhag par rnam par bam pa'i bsam gtan dag la snyoms par 'jug par bya'o snyam mo/_/des lhag par rnam par bam pa'i bsam gtan dag la snyoms par 'jug 
[\[38a.4\]]par brtsams te/_de rnam par bam pa'i bsam gtan dag la snyoms par zhugs pa na/_zhal dang shangs nas dbugs rngub pa dang /_'byung ba bgags so/_/de'i zhal dang shangs nas dbugs rngub pa dang /_'byung ba bgags pas rlung thams cad snyan gnyis kyi 
[\[38a.5\]]bu gnas rgyus par gyur to/_/dper na mgar bas sbud pa bus na rlung thams cad kyi rgyun grwa gnyis kyi bu gnas rgyu ba de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' rnam par bam pa'i bsam gtan dag la snyoms par zhugs pa na rlung thams cad kyi rgyun snyan gnyis kyi bu gnas 
[\[38a.6\]]rgyus par gyur kyang de zhum pa ma yin gyi/_brtson 'grus brtsams pa yin zhes bya ba la sogs pa ni snga ma bzhin no/_/de nas byang chub sems dpa' de 'di snyam du dgongs te/_ma la bdag gis lhag par yang rnam par bam pa'i bsam gtan dag la snyoms par 'jug 
[\[38a.7\]]par bya'o snyam mo/_/des lhag par rnam par bam pa'i bsam gtan dag la snyoms par 'jug par brtsams te/_de rnam par bam pa'i bsam gtan dag la snyoms par zhugs pa na/_zhal dang shangs nas dbugs rngub pa dang 'byung ba bgags so/_/de'i zhal dang 
[\[38b\]]
[\[38b.1\]]shangs nas dbugs rngub pa dang 'byung ba bgags pas phyal gyi nang thams cad rgyas par gyur/_shin tu rgyas par gyur te/_dper na mgar ba mkhas pas sbud pa bus na gang bar gyur/_shin tu gang bar gyur pa de bzhin du de'i tshe/_byang chub sems dpa' rnam par

[\[38b.4\]]pa na/_zhal dang shangs nas dbugs rngub pa dang 'byung ba bgags so/_/de'i zhal dang shangs nas dbugs rngub pa dang 'byung ba bgags pa dang /_de'i tshe dbu la dbu'i tshor ba shin tu mi bzad pa skyes so/_/dper na mi stobs dang ldan pas mi stobs shin tu chung

[\[71a.2\]]mngon du yang dag par dga' bar 'gyur ba dang /_mgu bar 'gyur ba'i gtam rnam pa sna tshogs mdzad nas phyogs gcig tu 'dug go_/phyogs gcig tu 'dug pa dang /_bcom ldan 'das kyis lteng rgyas 'od srung ral pa can la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to/_/'od srung 
[\[71a.3\]]gal te khyod la mi bde ba med na khyod kyi me khang du nga zhag gcig cig 'dug go/_/dge sbyong chen po bdag la mi bde ba ma mchis na/_'on kyang de na klu gdug pa zhig gnas te des khyod tho btsams par 'gyur du mchi'o/_/'od srung khyod kyis re zhig gnang bar gyis shig 
[\[71a.4\]]dang tho btsams par mi 'gyur ro/_/dge sbyong chen po gal te khyod la mtho mi 'tsham na dge sbyong chen po ci bde ba kho nar bzhugs shig_/de nas bcom ldan 'das lteng rgyas 'od srung ral pa can gyi me khang gang na ba der gshegs te byon nas me khang gi phyi rol du zhabs bsil 
[\[71a.5\]]nas me khang gi nang du zhugs te/_nyid kyis rtswa'i gding ba bshams nas sku drang por bsrang ste skyil mo krung bcas nas dran pa mngon du phyogs par bzhag ste bzhugs so/_/klu gdug pas bcom ldan 'das rgyang ring po nas mthong ngo /_/mthong nas kyang khros pa'i mthus dud 
[\[71a.6\]]pa phyung ngo /_/bcom ldan 'das kyis kyang rdzu 'phrul gyi mthus dud pa phyung ngo /_/klu gdug pas khro ba'i mthus 'bar bar byas so/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis kyang me'i khams la snyoms par zhugs so/_/de nas klu gdug pa khros pa'i mthu dang /_bcom ldan 'das kyi 
[\[71a.7\]]rdzu 'phrul gyi mthus me khang thams cad 'bar rab tu 'bar kun tu rab tu 'bar te/_me lce gcig tu gyur pas sreg par byed do/_/lteng rgyas 'od srung ral pa can mtshan mo rgyu skar la lta ba na me khang thams cad 'bar rab tu 'bar/_kun tu rab tu 'bar te/_me lce gcig 
[\[71b\]]
[\[71b.1\]]tu gyur nas sreg pa mthong ngo /_/mthong nas kyang 'di snyam du sems te/_dge sbyong chen po gau ta ma 'di ltar mdzes pa klu gdug pas thal bar byas par ma gyur gyur grang /_kyi hud nyon mongs bdag gi tshig ma nyan to snyam mo/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das 'di snyam du dgongs te/_nga lteng rgyas 
[\[71b.2\]]'od srung ral pa can 'khor dang bcas pas phyas spyad kyis ma la ngas rdzu 'phrul ji lta bur mngon par 'du bya ba la sems mnyam par bzhag na mes klu gdug pa'i me zil gyis mnan la/_des kyang de'i lus la gnod par mi byed cing de dul bar byas nas lhung bzed kyi nang du bcug ste/_
[\[71b.3\]]lteng rgyas 'od srung ral pa can de la sbyin pa de lta bu zhig bya'o snyam mo/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis rdzu 'phrul ji lta bu mngon par 'du mdzad pa la thugs mnyam par bzhag na mes klu gdug pa'i me zil gyis mnan la/_des kyang de'i lus la gnod pa mi byed pa de lta bu mngon par 
[\[71b.4\]]'du mdzad de/_de dul bar mkhyen nas lhung bzed du bcug ste/_lteng rgyas 'od srung gang na ba der bsnams te gshegs so/_/lteng rgyas 'od srung ral pa can gyis bcom ldan 'das rgyang ring po nas mthong ngo /_/mthong nas kyang bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to/_/
[\[71b.5\]]dge sbyong chen po gson pa lta zhig_/'od srung gson no/_/dge sbyong chen po khyod kyi lhung bzed na bsnams te byon pa ci/_'od srung klu gdug pa gang gi mthus khyod kyi me khang du mir gyur pa/_rdzu 'phrul dang mi ldan pa gnas su mi ster ba de ngas btul nas lhung bzed du bcug nas 
[\[71b.6\]]khyer te 'ongs so/_/de nas lteng rgyas 'od srung ral pa can 'di snyam du sems te/_'di ltar dge sbyong chen po rdzu 'phrul che zhing mthu che ba yin te ya mtshan che mod kyi bdag cag kyang dgra bcom pa yin no snyam mo/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das lteng rgyas 'od srung ral pa can gyi bsti 
[\[71b.7\]]gnas kyi nags tshang tshing na bzhugs so/_/de'i tshe lteng rgyas 'od srung ral pa can gyi bsti gnas na bram ze'i khye'u lnga brgya tsam me la yongs su spyod pa re re'i me thab gsum gsum ste/_/me thab stong lnga brgya yod do/_/de nas bram ze'i khye'u de rnams me la yongs su spyod par 'dod 
[\[72a\]]
[\[72a.1\]]na me spar bar ma nus so/_/de nas bram ze'i khye'u de rnams lteng rgyas 'od srung ral pa can gang na ba der song ste phyin nas/_lteng rgyas 'od srung ral pa can la 'di skad ces smras so/_/mkhan po mkhyen par mdzod cig_/'dir bdag cag zhugs la yongs su spyod par 
[\[72a.2\]]'tshal na zhugs de spar bar rngo ma thogs lags so/_/de nas lteng rgyas 'od srung ral pa can 'di snyam du sems te/_dge sbyong chen po bdag gi nye 'khor na gnas na de'i mthu zhig ma byung grang snyam mo/_/de nas lteng rgyas 'od srung ral pa can bcom ldan 'das gang na ba der song ste 
[\[72a.3\]]phyin nas/_bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to/_/dge sbyong chen po mkhyen par mdzod cig_/'dir bdag gi bram ze'i khye'u lnga brgya tsam me la yongs su spyod par 'tshal na/_me de spar bar rngo ma thogs nas bdag 'di snyam du sems te/_bdag gi nye 'khor na dge 
[\[72a.4\]]sbyong chen po bzhugs na de'i mthu zhig ma byung grang snyam bgyid do/_/'od srung me spar bar 'dod dam dge sbyong chen po spar du gsol/_de nas 'di ltar sangs rgyas rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi mthu dang /_lha rnams kyi lha'i mthus me de rang nyid 'bar bar gyur to/_/de nas lteng 
[\[72a.5\]]rgyas 'od srung ral pa can 'di snyam du sems te/_'di ltar dge sbyong chen po rdzu 'phrul che zhing mthu che ba yin te ya mtshan che mod kyi/_bdag kyang dgra bcom pa yin no snyam mo/_/de nas bram ze'i khye'u de rnams me la yongs su spyad nas me gsad par 'dod na gsad par ma nus so/_/
[\[72a.6\]]de nas bram ze'i khye'u de rnams lteng rgyas 'od srung ral pa can gang na ba der dong ste lhags nas lteng rgyas 'od srung ral pa can la 'di skad ces smras so/_/mkhan po mkhyen par mdzod cig_/'dir bdag cag zhugs la yongs su spyad nas zhugs dgum par 'tshal na dgum 
[\[72a.7\]]pa'i rngo ma thogs lags so/_/de nas lteng rgyas 'od srung ral pa can 'di snyam du sems te/_dge slong chen po bdag gi nye 'khor na gnas na de'i mthu zhig ma byung grang snyam mo/_/de nas lteng rgyas 'od srung ral pa can bcom ldan 'das gang na ba der dong ste phyin nas bcom ldan 'das 
[\[72b\]]
[\[72b.1\]]la 'di skad ces gsol to/_/dge sbyong chen po mkhyen par mdzad du gsol/_/'dir bdag gi bram ze'i khye'u rnams zhugs la yongs su spyad nas zhugs dgum par 'tshal na dgum pa'i rngo ma thogs nas bdag 'di snyam du sems te/_/bdag gi nye 'khor na dge sbyong 
[\[72b.2\]]chen po bzhugs na de'i mthu zhig ma byung grang snyam bgyid do/_/'od srung me de bsad dam/_dge sbyong chen po dgum du gsol/_de nas 'di ltar sangs rgyas rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi mthu dang /_lha rnams kyi lha'i mthus me de rang nyid shi'o/_/de nas lteng rgyas 'od 
[\[72b.3\]]srung ral pa can 'di snyam du sems te/_'di ltar dge sbyong chen po ni rdzu 'phrul che zhing mthu che ba yin te ya mtshan che mod kyi bdag kyang dgra bcom pa yin no snyam mo/_/de nas yang dus gzhan zhig na lteng rgyas 'od srung ral pa can rang nyid me la yongs su spyod par 'dod na spar bar 
[\[72b.4\]]ma nus so zhes bya ba nas/_bdag kyang dgra bcom pa yin no zhes bya ba'i bar du gong ma bzhin no snyam mo/_/de bzhin du yongs su spyad nas gsad par 'dod na zhes bya ba nas/_bdag kyang dgra bcom pa yin no zhes bya ba'i bar du gong ma bzhin no/_/de nas dus gzhan zhig na 
[\[72b.5\]]lteng rgyas 'od srung ral pa can gyi me khang thams cad 'bar rab tu 'bar/_kun tu rab tu 'bar te me lce gcig tu gyur nas sreg par byed pa de la lteng rgyas 'od srung ral pa can 'khor dang bcas pa dang /_gzhan yang skye bo'i tshogs chen po rnams kyis bsgrims kyang 
[\[72b.6\]]gsad par ma nus so/_/de nas lteng rgyas 'od srung ral pa can 'di snyam du sems te/_bdag gi nye 'khor na dge sbyong chen po gnas na de'i mthu zhig byung bar ma gyur grang snyam mo/_/de nas lteng rgyas 'od srung ral pa can bcom ldan 'das gang na ba der song ste phyin nas 
[\[72b.7\]]bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to/_/dge sbyong chen po mkhyen par mdzad du gsol/_dge sbyong chen po 'dir bdag gi me khang thams cad 'bar rab tu 'bar/_kun tu rab tu 'bar te/_me lce gcig tu gyur nas sreg par bgyid pa de la bdag 'khor dang bcas pa 
[\[73a\]]
[\[73a.1\]]dang /_gzhan yang skye bo'i tshogs chen pos bsgrims na zhugs dgum pa'i rngo ma thogs nas bdag 'di snyam du sems te/_bdag gi nye 'khor na dge sbyong chen po bzhugs na de'i mthu zhig byung bar ma gyur grang snyam bgyid do/_/'od srung me de zhi bar bya'am/_dge sbyong 
[\[73a.2\]]chen po zhi bar mdzad du gsol/_de nas 'di ltar sangs rgyas rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi mthu dang /_lha rnams kyi lha'i mthus me khang de rang nyid zhi bar gyur to/_/de nas lteng rgyas 'od srung ral pa can 'di snyam du sems te/_'di ltar dge sbyong chen po rdzu 'phrul che zhing mthu che ba 
[\[73a.3\]]yin te ya mtshan che mod kyi bdag kyang dgra bcom pa yin no snyam mo/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das lteng rgyas 'od srung ral pa can gyi bsti gnas kyi nags tshang tshing na bzhugs so/_/de nas rgyal po chen po bzhi po dag kha dog 'phags pas mtshan mo la bab pa na bcom ldan 'das 
[\[73a.4\]]gang na ba der dong ste lhags nas bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs la mgo bos phyag byas te de rnams dper na me'i phung po 'bar rab tu 'bar/_kun tu rab tu 'bar te/_me lce gcig tu gyur nas sreg par byed pa bzhi mthong ngo /_/mthong nas kyang 'di snyam du sems te/_dge 
[\[73a.5\]]sbyong chen po yang me la yongs su spyod de 'di ltar de'i spyan snga na me'i phung po 'bar rab tu 'bar/_kun tu rab tu 'bar te/_me lce gcig tu gyur nas sreg par byed pa bzhi yod do snyam mo/_de nas lteng rgyas 'od srung ral pa can nang par sngar langs te bcom ldan 'das gang 
[\[73a.6\]]na ba der song ste phyin nas bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to/_/dge sbyong chen po mkhyen par mdzad du gsol/_'dir bdag ma dang sum rgyu skar rnams la lta ba na dge sbyong chen po'i spyan snga na zhugs kyi phung po 'bar rab tu 'bar/_kun tu rab tu 'bar te/_me lce 
[\[73a.7\]]gcig tu gyur te sreg par byed pa bzhi mthong ngo /_/mthong nas kyang 'di snyam du sems te/_dge sbyong chen po yang me la yongs su spyod do/_/'di ltar de'i spyan snga na me'i phung po 'bar rab tu 'bar/_kun tu rab tu 'bar te/_me lce gcig tu gyur nas sreg par bgyid pa 
[\[73b\]]
[\[73b.1\]]bzhi mchis so snyam mo/_/'od srung cing me la yongs su spyod dam/_nga me la yongs su mi spyod de/_mkhas pa kun nas nyon mongs las/_/sems ni yongs su zlog byed kyi/_/me 'am yang na chu dag la/_/yongs bsten yon tan ci zhig yod/_/de lta mod kyi 'dir mdang sum 
[\[73b.2\]]rgyal po chen po bzhi po dag kha dog 'phags pas mtshan mo la bab pa na nga gang na ba der 'ongs te lhags nas nga'i zhabs gnyis la mgo bos phyag byas te de rnams dper na me'i phung po 'bar rab tu 'bar/_kun tu rab tu 'bar te/_me lce gcig tu gyur nas sreg par byed pa bzhi po lta 
[\[73b.3\]]bu'i lus kyis phyogs gcig tu 'dug pa yin no/_/de nas lteng rgyas 'od srung ral pa can 'di snyam du sems te/_'di ltar dge sbyong chen po rdzu 'phrul che zhing mthu che ba yin te ya mtshan che mod kyi bdag kyang dgra bcom pa yin no snyam mo/_/de nas lha rnams kyi dbang po brgya 
[\[73b.4\]]byin kha dog 'phags pas mtshan mo la bab pa na bcom ldan 'das gang na ba der song ste phyin nas bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs gnyis la mgo bos phyag byas te/_de dper na me'i phung po 'bar rab tu 'bar/_kun tu rab tu 'bar te/_me lce gcig tu gyur pa bzhi po 
[\[73b.5\]]de dag pas ches lhag cing ches 'phags la ches bzang ba lta bu'i lus kyis phyogs gcig tu 'dug go/_/lteng rgyas 'od srung ral pa can de nyid kyi mtshan mo rgyu skar rnams la lta ba na/_bcom ldan 'das kyi spyan snga na me'i phung po 'bar zhes bya ba nas/_dge sbyong chen po rdzu 
[\[73b.6\]]'phrul che zhing mthu che ba yin te/_ya mtshan che mod kyi bdag kyang dgra bcom pa yin no snyam mo zhes bya ba'i bar du gong ma bzhin no/_/de nas mi mjed kyi bdag po tshangs pa kha dog 'phags pas mtshan mo la bab pa na bcom ldan 'das gang na ba der song ste phyin nas/_bcom ldan 
[\[73b.7\]]'das kyi zhabs la mgo bos phyag byas te/_de dper na me'i phung po bzhi po de dag nyid snga ma bzhin du sreg pa zhes bya ba nas/_me'i phung po chen po lnga po de me lce gcig tu gyur nas sreg par byed do zhes bya ba'i bar de dag pas kyang ches lhag cing ches 'phags la ches bzang ba 
[\[74a\]]
[\[74a.1\]]lta bu'i lus kyis phyogs gcig tu 'dug go/_/lteng rgyas 'od srung ral pa can de nyid kyi mtshan mo rgyu skar rnams la lta ba na bcom ldan 'das kyi spyan snga na me'i phung po chen po 'bar zhes bya ba nas/_dge sbyong chen po rdzu 'phrul che zhing mthu che ba yin te/_ya mtshan che 
[\[74a.2\]]mod kyi bdag kyang dgra bcom pa yin no snyam mo zhes bya ba'i bar du gong ma bzhin no/_/de'i tshe lteng rgyas 'od srung ral pa can gyi 'dug gnas na mchod sbyin zhag bdun pa byed pa la bab bo/_/de nas lteng rgyas 'od srung ral pa can 'di snyam du sems te gal te mag d+hA'i mi rnams kyis dge sbyong 
[\[74a.3\]]chen po 'di ltar rdzu 'phrul che zhing mthu che ba yin par shes na de nyid la bya ba byed par sems kyi bdag la ni de lta ma yin pa'i gnas yod kyis/_kye ma dge sbyong chen po zhag bdun zhig 'di nas gzhan du song yang ci ma rung snyam mo/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyi thugs kyis lteng rgyas 'od srung ral 
[\[74a.4\]]pa can gyi sems mkhyen nas zhag bdun gzhan du bzhud do/_/de'i tshe zhag bdun du lteng rgyas 'od srung ral pa can la rnyed pa dang /_bkur sti chen po byung nas/_bza' ba dang bca' ba rab tu mang po dang ldan/_skye bo'i tshogs chen po yang dbang du gyur to/_/de nas lteng rgyas 'od srung 
[\[74a.5\]]ral pa can gyi 'dug gnas na mchod sbyin zhag bdun du byas zin pa dang 'di snyam du sems te/_zhag bdun po 'di la bdag la rnyed pa dang /_bkur sti chen po byung nas bza' ba dang /_bca' ba rab tu mang po dang ldan/_skye bo'i tshogs chen po yang dbang du gyur gyis kye 
[\[74a.6\]]ma da ni dge sbyong chen po 'ongs kyang ci ma rung bdag gis de yang de la bsod snyoms spyod du gzhug go snyam mo/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyi thugs kyis lteng rgyas 'od srung ral pa can gyi sems mkhyen nas lteng rgyas 'od srung ral pa can gang na ba der gshegs so/_/lteng rgyas 'od 
[\[74a.7\]]srung ral pa can gyis bcom ldan 'das rgyang ring po nas mthong ngo /_/mthong nas kyang bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to/_/dge sbyong chen po byon tam/_'od srung 'ongs so/_/dge sbyong chen po khyod ci'i slad du zhag bdun 'di nas gzhan du bzhud lags/_'od srung 
[\[74b\]]
[\[74b.1\]]khyod kyi 'dug gnas na mchod sbyin zhag bdun du byed pa la bab nas 'di snyam du gal te ma ga d+hA'i mi rnams kyis dge sbyong chen po ni 'di ltar rdzu 'phrul che zhing 'di ltar mthu che ba yin no bar shes na de kho na la bya ba byed par sems kyi bdag la ni de lta ma yin pa'i gnas 'di yod par 'gyur gyis 
[\[74b.2\]]kye ma dge sbyong chen po zhag bdun zhig 'di nas gzhan du song yang ci ma rung snyam sems pa ma yin nam/_/nga'i thugs kyis khyod kyi sems shes nas zhag bdun 'di nas gzhan du song ngo /_/'o na ci'i slad du 'dir byon lags/_'od srung khyod kyi mchod sbyin zhag bdun du byas zin 
[\[74b.3\]]nas 'di snyam du bdag la zhag bdun du rnyed pa dang /_bkur sti chen po byung zhing bza' ba dang bca' ba rab tu mang po dang yang ldan/_skye bo'i tshogs chen po yang dbang du gyur gyis kye ma dge sbyong chen po 'ongs kyang ci ma rung bdag gis de yang de la yongs su longs spyod du gzhug go 
[\[74b.4\]]snyam du sems pa ma yin nam dge sbyong chen po de lta lags so/_/nga'i thugs kyis khyod kyi sems shes nas nga 'ongs pa yin no/_/dge sbyong chen po ci bde ba kho nar longs spyad du gsol/_de nas lteng rgyas 'od srung ral pa can 'di snyam du sems te/_'di ltar dge sbyong chen po 
[\[74b.5\]]rdzu 'phrul che zhing mthu che ba yin te/_ya mtshan che mod kyi bdag kyang dgra bcom pa yin no snyam mo/_/de nas lteng rgyas 'od srung ral pa can gyis bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to/_/dge sbyong chen po de'i slad du bdag gi nye 'khor du bzhugs shig dang bdag gis khyod la 
[\[74b.6\]]chos dang 'thun pa'i 'tsho ba sbyar ro/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis lteng rgyas 'od srung ral pa can la cang mi gsung bas gnang ngo /_/de nas lteng rgyas 'od srung ral pa can gyis bcom ldan 'das kyi ched du rang kho nas g.yos byas nas bcom ldan 'das gang na ba der song ste phyin nas bcom 
[\[74b.7\]]ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to/_/dge sbyong chen po gdugs tshod lags shing mchis na dge sbyong chen po da de'i dus la bab par dgongs su gsol/_'od srung de'i phyir song shig nga yang 'ong ngo /_/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis lteng rgyas 'od srung ral pa can song nas ring po ma 
[\[75a\]]
[\[75a.1\]]lon par mkhyen nas ji ltar thugs mnyam par bzhag na shing 'dzam bu zhes bya ba gang gis 'dzam bu gling du gdags pa de las kha dog dang dri dang ro dang ldan pa'i 'dzam bu'i snye ma rnams kyis lhung bzed bkang nas bsnams te/_lteng rgyas 'od srung ral pa can gyi bsti gnas 
[\[75a.2\]]gang na ba der gshegs pa de lta bu'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs te byon nas gdan bshams pa nyid la bzhugs so/_/de nas de'i 'og tu lteng rgyas 'od srung ral pa can 'ongs nas lteng rgyas 'od srung ral pa can gyis bcom ldan 'das bsti gnas kyi gdan bshams pa nyid la 
[\[75a.3\]]bzhugs pa mthong ngo /_/mthong nas kyang 'di skad ces gsol to/_/dge sbyong chen po byon tam/_'od srung 'ongs so/_/dge sbyong chen po khyod kyi lhung bzed na ci mchis/_'dir ngas 'od srung khyod song nas ring po ma lon par mkhyen nas/_ji ltar thugs mnyam par bzhag 
[\[75a.4\]]na shing 'dzam bu zhes bya ba gang gis 'dzam bu gling du gdags pa de las kha dog dang dri dang ro dang ldan pa'i 'dzam bu'i snye ma rnams kyis lhung bzed bkang nas 'dir phyin pa de lta bu'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs kyis gal te 'dod na longs spyod cig_/dge sbyong chen po ci bde ba kho nar 
[\[75a.5\]]'tshal lags so/_/de nas lteng rgyas 'od srung ral pa can 'di snyam du sems te/_'di ltar dge sbyong chen po rdzu 'phrul che zhing mthu che ba yin te/_ya mtshan che mod kyi bdag kyang dgra bcom pa yin no snyam mo/_/shing 'dzam bu zhes bya ba gang gis 'dzam bu'i gling du gdags pa 
[\[75a.6\]]de dang /_ha cang mi ring ba zhig na a ru ra'i nags dang /_bil ba'i nags dang ka pid tha'i nags dang /_skyu ru ra 'i nags kyang de bzhin no/_/byang gi sgra mi snyan gyi gling du gshegs nas ma rmos ma btab pa'i 'bras sA lus lhung bzed bkang ste bsnams nas lteng rgyas 'od srung ral pa can gyi 
[\[75a.7\]]bsti gnas gang na ba der gshegs te byon nas gdan bshams pa nyid la bzhugs so/_/de nas de'i 'og tu lteng rgyas 'od srung ral pa can 'ongs nas lteng rgyas 'od srung ral pa can gyis bcom ldan 'das bsti gnas kyi gdan bshams pa nyid la bzhugs pa mthong ngo /_/mthong nas kyang 'di skad ces 
[\[75b\]]
[\[75b.1\]]gsol to/_/dge sbyong chen po byon tam/_'od srung 'ongs so/_/dge sbyong chen po khyod kyi lhung bzed na ci mchis 'dir ngas 'od srung khyod song nas ring po ma lon par mkhyen nas ji ltar thugs mnyam par bzhag na byang gi sgra mi snyan gyi gling du gshegs nas ma rmos ma btab pa'i 'bras sA lu'i 
[\[75b.2\]]'bras lhung bzed bkang nas 'dir phyin pa de lta bu'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs kyis gal te 'dod na longs spyod cig_/dge slong chen po ci bde ba kho nar 'tshal lags so/_/de nas lteng rgyas 'od srung ral pa can 'di snyam du sems te/_'di ltar dge sbyong chen po rdzu 'phrul che 
[\[75b.3\]]zhing mthu che ba yin te ya mtshan che mod kyi bdag kyang dgra bcom pa yin no snyam mo/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis lteng rgyas 'od srung ral pa can las bsod snyoms bzhes nas phyogs gzhan zhig tu bzhugs nas zhal zas kyi bya ba mdzad do/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das la chus chu'i

[\[89b.1\]]de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa smras pa/_ston pa dgongs yul mtha' yas pa/_/skyes bu'i dbang po mkhyen mthar byon/_/bcom ldan srid pa mthar mdzad pas/_/grogs po nye rgyal pang nas skyes/_/'od ma'i tshal du ma phyin par/_/de ltar mchi ba gzigs gyur nas/_/
[\[89b.2\]]tshogs mchog dbus su de gnyis ni/_/'di gnyis nga yi nyan thos kyi/_/zung mchog 'gyur zhes des bstan te/_/gcig ni rdzu 'phrul ldan pa'i mchog_/cig shos shes rab ldan pa yi/_/mchog tu 'gyur bar lung bstan mdzad/_/de nas kun tu rgyu nye rgyal dang /_
[\[89b.3\]]pang nas skyes gnyis bcom ldan 'das ga la ba der dong ste phyin nas bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs gnyis la mgo bos phyag 'tshal te bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to/_/btsun pa bdag cag legs par gsungs pa'i chos 'dul ba la rab tu byung ba dang /_
[\[89b.4\]]bsnyen par rdzogs pa dge slong gi dngos por gnang na bdag cag bcom ldan 'das kyi spyan sngar tshangs par spyod pa spyad par bgyi'o/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis de gnyis la dge slong tshur shog ces bya bas rab tu byung zhing dge slong dag tshur shog_/tshangs par spyad pa spyod cig 
[\[89b.5\]]ces bka' stsal pa dang /_de dag bcom ldan 'das kyi bka'i mjug kho nar skra byi ba dang /_snam sbyar gyon par gyur te lag na lhung bzed thogs pa skra dang kha spu zhag bdun skyes pa tsam dang /_dge slong bsnyen par rdzogs nas lo brgya lon pa'i spyod lam lta bur gnas 
[\[89b.6\]]par gyur to/_/yang smras pa/_de bzhin gshegs pas tshur shog bka' stsal pas/_/skra byi snam sbyar gyon pa'i lus ldan zhing /_/mod la dbang po rab zhir gnas gyur te/_/sangs rgyas dgongs pas lus gzugs bkab par gyur/_/de nas dge slong rab tu mang po dag 
[\[89b.7\]]snga dro sham thabs bgos/_lhung bzed dang chos gos thogs te/_rgyal po'i khab tu bsod snyoms la zhugs pa dang /_de nas rgyal po'i khab kyi mi gang dag yang dag rgyal ba can gyi mu stegs can gyi gnas rab tu byung zhing bsnyen par rdzogs nas ring po ma lon pas yid ma 
[\[90a\]]
[\[90a.1\]]rangs shing mngon par mi dga' ba de dag gis dge slong de dang de dag mthong nas co 'dri zhing tshigs su bcad pa smras pa/_sangs rgyas ma ga d+hA dag gi/_/grong mchog rgyal po'i khab gshegs pas/_/yang dag rgyal ba'i kun khrid na/_/phyin chad ci zhig 
[\[90a.2\]]bkri bar bya/_/des dge slong rnams spa gong zhing mi dga' ba dang /_mi 'jigs pa med par gyur to/_/de nas dge slong rab tu mang po dag rgyal po'i khab tu bsod snyoms rgyus nas zas kyi bya ba byas te/_phyi dro bsod snyoms mi len pas lhung bzed dang chos gos mkhos su 
[\[90a.3\]]phab nas/_rkang pa gnyis bkrus te/_sdug bsngal zhing yid mi bde bas bcom ldan 'das ga la ba der dong ste phyin nas bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs gnyis la mgo bos phyag 'tshal te phyogs gcig tu 'khod do/_/phyogs gcig tu 'khod nas bcom ldan 'das la dge 
[\[90a.4\]]slong rab tu mang po dag gis 'di skad ces gsol to/_/btsun pa 'di na bdag cag dge slong rab tu mang po dag snga dro sham thabs 'tshal/_lhung bzed dang chos gos dag thogs te/_rgyal po'i khab tu bsod snyoms la zhugs pa dang /_de nas rgyal po'i khab kyi mi gang dag 
[\[90a.5\]]yang dag rgyal ba can gyi mu stegs can gyi gnas nas rab tu byung zhing bsnyen par rdzogs nas ring po ma lags pas yid ma rangs shing mngon par mi dga' ba de dag gis dge slong de dang /_de dag mthong nas co 'dri zhing tshigs su bcad pa smras pa/_sangs rgyas ma ga d+hA dag 
[\[90a.6\]]gi/_grong mchog rgyal po'i khab gshegs pas/_/yang dag rgyal ba'i kun khrid na/_/phyin chad ci zhig bkri bar bya/_/zhes mchi nas des bdag cag rnams spa gong zhing mi dga' ba dang /_mi 'jigs pa ma mchis par gyur to/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_
[\[90a.7\]]dge slong dag rgyal po'i khab kyi mi de skad zer ba rnams la 'di skad ces/_'di la dpa' chen bde gshegs dag_/dam pa'i chos kyis khrid mdzad de/_/de dag chos kyis khrid mdzad la/_/mkhas pa su zhig mi bzod byed/_/ces brjod na rgyal po'i khab kyi mi de skad zer ba 
[\[90b\]]
[\[90b.1\]]rnams kyi rlom pa tsam mam/_spobs pa tsam gang yan pa de thams cad kyi thams cad du med par gyur to/_/de nas dge slong rab tu mang po dag snga dro sham thabs bgos/_lhung bzed dang chos gos thogs te rgyal po'i khab tu bsod snyoms la zhugs pa dang /_de nas rgyal po'i khab kyi mi gang dag 
[\[90b.2\]]yang dag rgyal ba can gyi mu stegs can gyi gnas nas rab tu byung zhing bsnyen par rdzogs nas ring po ma lon pas yid ma rangs shing mngon par mi dga' ba de dag gis dge slong de dang de dag mthong nas co 'dri zhing tshigs su bcad pa smras pa/_sangs rgyas ma ga d+hA dag gi_/
[\[90b.3\]]grong mchog rgyal po'i khab gshegs pas/_/yang dag rgyal ba'i kun khrid na/_/phyin chad ci zhig bkri bar bya/_/de nas dge slong zhig gis de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa smras pa/_'di na dpa' chen bde gshegs dag_/dam pa'i chos kyis khrid mdzad de/_/de dag chos kyis khrid 
[\[90b.4\]]mdzad la/_/mkhas pa su zhig bzod mi byed/_/de skad ces smras pa dang /_rgyal po'i khab kyi mi de skad zer ba rnams kyi rlom pa tsam mam/_spobs pa tsam gang yin pa de thams cad kyi thams cad du med par gyur to/_/de nas sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rgyal bu rgyal byed kyi tshal mgon 
[\[90b.5\]]med zas sbyin gyi kun dga' ra ba na bzhugs so/_/ko sa la'i rgyal po gsal rgyal gyis rgyal po zas gtsang la spring yig bskur ba/_lha dgyes pa bskyed du gsol/_khyod kyi sras kyis bdud rtsi thob nas bdud rtsis 'gro ba tshim par byed do zhes spring ba rgyal po zas 
[\[90b.6\]]gtsang gis thos nas pho nya btang ba las de de nyid du rab tu byung ste/_pho nya gang dang gang btang ba de dang de dag de nyid du legs par gsungs pa'i chos 'dul ba nyid la rab tu byung bar gyur to/_/rgyal po zas gtsang lag pa 'gram pa la gtad nas pho nya gang dang gang btang 
[\[90b.7\]]ba de dang de dag de nyid du legs par gsungs pa'i chos 'dul ba nyid la rab tu byung ngo snyam nas sems khong du chud cing 'dug pa 'char kas mthong nas smras pa/_lha khyod ci'i slad du phyag la zhal gtad nas thugs mi dgyes shing bzhugs/_des smras pa/_'char ka 
[\[91a\]]
[\[91a.1\]]gang gi tshe/_gzhon nu don thams cad grub pa dga' ba spyod pa de'i tshe kho bos spring yig skyel ba'i mi btang ba las de dag kho bo'i gan du 'ongs nas gzhon nu don thams cad grub pa bzhugs pa 'di dang 'dis bzhugs so/_/zhes smra'o/_/da ni kho bos spring yig skyel ba'i 
[\[91a.2\]]mi btang na 'ga' yang phrin yig khyer te mi 'ong na ji ltar sems khong du chud par mi 'gyur/_'char kas smras pa/_lha bdag mchis te bka' mchid 'tshal la slar mchi'o/_/rgyal po zas gtsang gis smras pa/_'char ka gang dang gang song ba de dang de'i bstan pa phun sum 
[\[91a.3\]]tshogs pa mthong nas de nyid du 'dug pas khyod kyang song na de nyid du 'dug par 'gyur ba'i gnas gang yin pa de yod do/_/'char kas smras pa/_lha slar mchis lags so/_/de nas rgyal po zas gtsang rang nyid kyis spring yig bris pa/_lhums su zhugs pa bzung nas su/_/
[\[91a.4\]]khyod ni bdag gis cher bskrun te/_/nyon mongs me rab 'bar bzhin du/_/rgyal ba ljon shing la bsten na/_/de nyid khyod kyi slob ma yi/_/yal ga lcug ma 'phel 'gyur bas/_gzhan ni bde bas dga' bar 'gyur/_/bdag ni sdug bsngal skal bar ldan/_/gzhan 
[\[91a.5\]]yang gang gis khyod ni ljon shing lcug phran mtshungs tsam na/_/bdag gi bu ni 'di yin snyam nas chung nas bskrun bskrun te/_/bu gcig yul mis 'bras bu yongs su spyad pa thob gyur na/_/de la yon tan 'bras bu mi ster khyod la snying rje med/_/gang tshe thog ma bltam 
[\[91a.6\]]pa na/_/nyid don bsgrubs nas gnyen rnams ni/_/shin tu che dang phan gdags zhes/_/khyod kyis dam bcas mdzad pas na/_/khyod kyis zhi gnas de brnyes na/_/bdag dang gnyen gyi tshogs rnams la/_/thugs brtses rjes su gzung mdzod de/_/gshegs 
[\[91a.7\]]par dgongs pa mdzad du gsol/_/zhes spring ba'i yi ge 'char kas khyer nas chas te rim gyis mnyan yod du phyin pa dang /_lam gyis dub pa ngal bsos nas bcom ldan 'das gang na ba der song ste phyin nas bcom ldan 'das rgyal po zas gtsang gis gsol ba'i 
[\[91b\]]
[\[91b.1\]]yi ge lags so/_/zhes bcom ldan 'das la yi ge phul ba dang /_bcom ldan 'das nyid kyis bzhes nas bklags so/_/bklags nas yi ge mtha' gcig tu bzhag pa dang /_'char kas gsol pa/_bcom ldan 'das ci lags/_ser skya'i gzhir mi bzhud dam/_
[\[91b.2\]]bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_'char ka 'dong ngo /_/'char ka sngon bcom ldan 'das dang lhag par 'dris pa yin pas de sngon gyi 'dris pa kho nas gsol pa/_bcom ldan 'das gal te mi bzhud dang bdag gi mthus bkri bar bgyi lags so/_/de nas bcom 
[\[91b.3\]]ldan 'das kyis byung ba 'di la tshigs su bcad de bka' stsal pa/_gang zhig yul gyi rgyal mor ma chags shing /_/sred 'gas bkri ba yod pa ma yin pa'i/_/sangs rgyas spyod yul mtha' yas mnga' ba de/_/gzhi med na ni gang gi gzhi yis bkri/_/gang zhig 
[\[91b.4\]]yul gyi rgyal mor ma chags shing /_/sred 'gas bkri ba yod pa ma yin pa'i/_/sangs rgyas mtha' yas pa yi rtsal mnga' de/_/gzhi med na ni gang gi gzhi yis/_bkri/_/gang zhig bcom nas brtsegs pa med gyur cing /_/'jig rten ma bcom [{]chung zad [,]cung zad [}]yod 
[\[91b.5\]]min pa/_/sangs rgyas spyod yul mtha' yas mnga' ba de/_/gzhi med na ni gang gi gzhi yis bkri/_/lan la spobs pa med nas gsol pa/_bcom ldan 'das mchi ste/_rgyal po zas gtsang ma la rig par bgyi'o/_/'char ka sangs rgyas kyi pho nya ni 'di 
[\[91b.6\]]lta bu ma yin no/_/'char kas gsol pa/_bcom ldan 'das ci 'dra ba zhig lags/_rab tu byung ba yin gyis khyod kyang rab tu byung shig_/bcom ldan 'das bdag gis rgyal po zas gtsang la slar mchi bar dam bcas lags so/_/bcom ldan

[\[92a.1\]]rnams kyis chos dang 'thun pa'i tshig lan 'ga' yang slar ma bzlog pas/_'char kas gsol pa/_bcom ldan 'das gal te de lta na rab tu 'byung lags so/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis tshe dang ldan pa shA ri'i bu la bka' stsal pa/_
[\[92a.2\]]shA ri'i bu 'char ka 'di ni yun ring po'i don dang /_phan pa dang /_bde ba'i phyir rab tu phyung shig_/btsun pa bka' bzhin 'tshal zhes tshe dang ldan pa shA ri'i bus bcom ldan 'das la mnyan nas 'char ka rab tu byung ste/_bsnyen par rdzogs par byas nas kun tu 
[\[92a.3\]]spyod pa'i chos dag de la rgyas par smras nas/_'char ka 'di ni khyod kyi bya ba yin te/_deng phan chad bud med dang lhan cig khang pa gcig tu nyal bar mi bya'o zhes bya bdag go/_/btsun pa bka' bzhin 'tshal zhes 'char kas tshe dang ldan pa shA ri'i bu'i rkang pa

[\[92a.5\]]bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_'char ka song la khyod mod kho nar rgyal po'i khab tu ma 'jug par sgor sdod la shAkya'i dge slong 'ongs so zhes brjod par bya'o/_/gal te nang du spyon cig zer na 'jug par bya'o/_/zhugs nas gal te shAkya'i dge slong 
[\[92a.6\]]gzhan yang yod dam zhes zer na/_yod do zhes brjod par bya'o/_/gal te gzhon nu don thams cad grub pa yang 'di 'dra zhing cha byad 'di 'dra 'am zhes 'dri na/_'di 'dra zhing cha byad 'di 'dra'o zhes brjod par bya'o/_/khyod kyis rgyal po'i khab tu yang gnas 
[\[92a.7\]]par mi bya'o/_/gal te gzhon nu don thams cad grub pa yang rgyal po'i khab tu gnas par mi byed dam zhes 'dri na/_mi bzhugs so/_/zhes brjod par bya'o/_/gal te gang du gnas par byed ces 'dri na/_dgon pa'am/_gtsug lag khang du'o zhes brjod 
[\[92b\]]
[\[92b.1\]]par bya'o/_/gal te gtsug lag khang ci 'dra zhes 'dri na/_rgyal bu rgyal byed kyi tshal gyi rnam pa bris te bstan par bya'o/_/gal te gzhon nu don thams cad grub pa byon nam zhes 'dri na/_byon no zhes brjod par bya'o/_/ji srid cig na byon zhes 'dri na/_zhag bdun

[\[92b.3\]]pa rnams kyi/_/sems bskyed pas ni thams cad 'grub/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis tshe dang ldan pa 'char ka de ji ltar skad cig de nyid la grong khyer ser skya'i gzhir song ba de ltar byin gyis brlabs pa dang /_tshe dang ldan pa 'char ka bcom ldan 'das la dad pa 
[\[92b.4\]]drag po skyes pa de rgyal po'i khab kyi sgor bsdad nas smras pa/_shes ldan dag rgyal po la shAkya'i dge slong mchis so zhes gsol cig_/de rnams kyis smras pa/_ci shAkya'i dge slong gzhan dag kyang yod dam/_shes ldan dag yod do/_/de rnams kyis 
[\[92b.5\]]rgyal po la smras pa/_shAkya'i dge slong mchis so/_/rgyal pos smras pa/_nang du thong shig_/de nang du zhugs pa rgyal po zas gtsang gis mthong nas smras pa/_'char ka rab tu byung ngam/_lha rab tu byung ngo /_/khyod slar 'ong ngo zhes smras pa ma yin nam/_
[\[92b.6\]]'di ci yin/_'char kas smras pa/_lha bcom ldan 'das kyis sangs rgyas kyi pho nya ni 'di lta bu ma yin no/_/zhes bka' stsal nas chos 'dul ba legs par gsungs pa'i phyir bdag rab tu byung mod kyi bdag rang gi dad pas kyang rab tu byung ngo /_/rgyal 
[\[92b.7\]]pos yan lag thams cad kyis 'khyud nas stan bshams pa nyid la 'dug tu bcug ste/_de'i don du chur btsags pa dang /_'bras bu btags pa gtsang sbra byas te zar bcug go/_/tshe dang ldan pa 'char ka mchog tu nye bar zhi ba dang /_dul bar gnas pa

[\[93a.1\]]dang ldan zhing ji skad rjes su bstan pa'i cho gas yongs su longs spyod do/_/rgyal pos de la brtags nas ngo mtshar skyes te smras pa/_'char ka ci gzhon nu don thams cad grub pa yang 'di 'dra bar nye bar zhi ba yin nam/_'char kas smras pa/_lha bdag gi 
[\[93a.2\]]dul ba dang nye bar zhi ba ni cung zad tsam lags so/_/dper ji lta bu ri rab dang yungs 'bru bzhin no/_/rgya mtsho dang rmig rjes bzhin no/_/nyi ma dang srin bu me khyer bzhin no/_/nam mkha' dang pad ma'i rtsa ba'i bu ga bzhin no/_/rgyal po zas gtsang gis smras pa/_'char 
[\[93a.3\]]ka gzhon nu don thams cad grub pa yang 'di 'dra zhing cha byad 'di 'dra'am/_lha 'di 'dra zhing cha byad kyang 'di 'dra'o/_/rgyal po zas gtsang brgya la nas sa la 'gyel ba dang /_chu mang du btab nas dbugs slar phyung ste smras pa/_'char ka gzhon nu don thams 
[\[93a.4\]]cad grub pa byon nam/_'char kas smras pa/_lha byon no/_/dus ji tsam na dgung bdun lon na'o/_/rgyal po zas gtsang gis blon po rnams la bsgo ba/_shes ldan gzhon nu don thams cad grub pa byon par 'gyur gyis/_rgyal po'i khab dang pho brang 
[\[93a.5\]]byi dor byos shig_/'char kas smras pa/_bcom ldan 'das ni pho brang du bzhugs par mi mdzad do/_/rgyal pos smras pa/_gang du bzhugs/_'char kas smras pa/_dgon pa'am/_gtsug lag khang du'o/_/rgyal po zas gtsang gis smras pa/_
[\[93a.6\]]'char ka gtsug lag khang ci 'dra ba yin/_tshe dang ldan pa 'char kas rgyal bu rgyal byed kyi tshal gyi rnam pa ri mor bris te bstan to/_/de nas rgyal pos blon po rnams la smras pa/_shes ldan dag n+ya gro d+hA'i kun dga' ra bar rgyal bu rgyal byed kyi tshal gyi rnam pa 
[\[93a.7\]]gtsug lag khang chen po bcu drug dang /_khang phran drug cu brtsig tu chug shig_/lha bka' bzhin 'tshal zhes blon po rnams kyis rgyal po zas gtsang las mnyan nas n+ya gro d+hA'i kun dga' ra bar rgyal bu rgyal byed kyi tshal gyi rnam pa gtsug lag khang chen po bcu drug dang /_
[\[93b\]]
[\[93b.1\]]khang phran drug cu brtsig tu bcug go/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis tshe dang ldan pa maud gal gyi bu chen po la 'di skad ces bka' stsal te/_maud gal gyi bu song la dge slong rnams la bcom ldan 'das ser skya'i gzhir gshegs te yab sras gdan 'dzom na/_khyod cag 
[\[93b.2\]]las su zhig lta bar spro ba de chos gos dag long shig ces bsgo shig_/btsun pa bka' bzhin 'tshal zhes tshe dang ldan pa maud gal gyi bu chen pos bcom ldan 'das las mnyan nas dge slong rnams la smras pa/_tshe dang ldan pa dag bcom ldan 'das ser skya'i gzhir gshegs 
[\[93b.3\]]te/_yab sras gdan 'dzom na/_khyed cag las su lta bar spro ba de chos gos dag long shig ces bka' stsal to/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das dul ba 'khor dul ba/_zhi ba 'khor zhi ba/_grol ba 'khor grol ba/_dbugs phyin pa/_'khor dbugs phyin pa/_
[\[93b.4\]]rnam par dul ba 'khor rnam par dul ba/_dgra bcom pa 'khor dgra bcom pa/_'dod chags dang bral ba 'khor 'dod chags dang bral ba/_mdzes pa 'khor mdzes pa/_khyu mchog ba lang gi tshogs kyis bskor ba lta bu/_glang po che glang po'i phrug gu'i 
[\[93b.5\]]tshogs kyis bskor ba lta bu/_seng ge mche ba can gyi tshogs kyis bskor ba lta bu/_ngang pa'i rgyal po ngang pa'i tshogs kyis bskor ba lta bu/_nam mkha' lding bya'i tshogs kyis bskor ba lta bu/_bram ze slob ma'i tshogs kyis bskor ba lta bu/_sman pa mkhas pa nad 
[\[93b.6\]]pa'i tshogs kyis bskor ba lta bu/_dpa' bo ru pa'i tshogs kyis bskor ba lta bu/_lam mkhan 'gron po'i tshogs kyis bskor ba lta bu/_ded dpon tshong pa'i tshogs kyis bskor ba lta bu/_khams kyi rgyal po blon po'i tshogs kyis bskor ba lta bu/_'khor los sgyur ba'i 
[\[93b.7\]]rgyal po bu stong gis bskor ba lta bu/_zla ba rgyu skar gyi tshogs kyis bskor ba lta bu/_nyi ma 'od zer stong gis bskor ba lta bu/_yul 'khor skyong dri za'i tshogs kyis bskor ba lta bu/_'phags skyes po grul bum gyi tshogs kyis bskor ba lta bu/_mig mi bzang klu'i tshogs 
[\[94a\]]
[\[94a.1\]]kyis bskor ba lta bu/_rnam thos kyi bu gnod sbyin gyi tshogs kyis bskor ba lta bu/_thags [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]ris lha ma yin gyi tshogs kyis bskor ba lta bu/_brgya byin sum cu rtsa gsum pa'i tshogs kyis bskor ba lta bu/_tshangs pa tshangs ris kyi tshogs 
[\[94a.2\]]kyis bskor ba lta bu/_rgya mtsho g.yo ba med pa lta bu/_sprin chu dang bcas pa lta bu/_glang po dam pa dregs pa dang bral ba ltar dbang po rnams shin tu dul bas spyod lam gyi tshul ma 'khrugs pasku skyes bu chen po'i mtshan sum cu rtsa gnyis kyis kun nas brgyan pa/_dpe byad 
[\[94a.3\]]bzang po brgyad cus rnam par mdzes par byas pa/_'od 'dom gang gis brgyan pa'i sku nyi ma'i 'od zer stong bas lhag pa'i 'od can/_rin po che'i ri bo 'gro ba ltar kun nas mdzes pa/_stobs bcu dang /_mi 'jigs pa bzhi dang /_ma 'dres pa'i dran pa nye bar gzhag pa 
[\[94a.4\]]gsum dang /_thugs rje chen po dang ldan pa/_dge slong gi dge 'dun chen po dang thabs cig tu 'bab chu ro hi ta kar byon to/_/rgyal po zas gtsang gis gzhon nu don thams cad grub pa 'bab chu ro hi ta kar byon to zhes thos so/_/thos nas kyang rgyal po zas gtsang gis grong khyer 
[\[94a.5\]]ser skya'i gzhi rdo ba dang gyo mo dang /_gseg ma rnams bsal nas tsan dan gyi chus chag chag btab/_dri shin tu zhim po'i pog por nye bar bshams/_dar gyi chun 'phyang mang po dpyangs te/_gang yang 'bab chu ro hi ta ka nas gang yang n+ya gro d+hA'i kun dga' ra ba de'i bar 
[\[94a.6\]]gyi lam shin tu mdzes par byas te/_sa phyogs yangs shing rgya che bar gdan rnam par bkra ba dag shom du bcug go/_/de nas 'bab chu ro hi ta ka la dge slong kha cig ni lag pa 'khru bar byed do/_/kha cig ni so shing 'dor bar byed do/_/kha cig ni khrus byed do/_/ser skya'i 
[\[94a.7\]]gzhi na gnas pa'i shAkya rnams kyis gzhon nu don thams cad grub pa gshegs so zhes thos so/_/thos nas kyang kha cig ni sngon gyi dge ba'i rtsa bas bskul/_kha cig ni ya mtshan skyes nas thams cad n+ya gro d+hA'i kun dga' ra ba nas byung ste/_ci yab sras kyi zhabs la phyag byed 
[\[94b\]]
[\[94b.1\]]dam/_'on te sras yab kyi zhabs la phyag byed pa blta'o snyam mo/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das 'di snyam du dgongs te/_gal te nga zhabs kyis ser skya'i gzhir gshegs na shAkya'i nga rgyal can rnams kye ma'o gzhon nu don thams cad grub pas dka' thub kyi nags su gshegs

[\[99a\]]
[\[99a.1\]]kyis kyang bcom ldan 'das kyi dgongs pa shes par 'gyur na/_brgya byin dang tshangs pa la sogs pa lha rnams kyis lta smos kyang ci dgos/_gang gi tshe 'jig rten las 'das pa'i thugs bskyed pa de'i tshe nyan thos chen po rnams kyi sems

[\[122a.6\]]pos khyim gyi bu dang /_tsha bo dang /_yang tsha dang /_sum tsha'i rgyud rgyal po lnga khri lnga stong grong khyer ser skya'i gzhir byung ste/_de rnams kyi tha ma ni shing rta bcu pa dang /_shing rta brgya pa dang /_shing rta dgu bcu dang /_shing rta khra bo dang /_shing rta rgyal dang /_brtan pa'i 
[\[122a.7\]]shing rta dang /_gzhu bcu pa dang /_gzhu brgya pa dang /_gzhu dgu bcu pa dang /_gzhu rgyal dang /_gzhu bkra dang /_gzhu brtan no/_/gau ta ma dag gzhu brtan la bu gnyis te/_seng ge'i 'gram dang /_seng ge'i sgra'o/_/gau ta ma dag 'dzam bu'i gling gi gzhu thogs ji snyed yod pa de rnams kyi 
[\[122b\]]
[\[122b.1\]]nang na seng ge'i 'gram mchog tu grags so/_/gau ta ma dag seng ge'i 'gram la bu bzhi ste/_zas gtsang dang /_zas dkar dang /_bre bo zas dang /_bdud rtsi'i zas so/_/bu mo gtsang ma dang /_dkar mo dang /_bye mo ma dang /_bdud rtsi ma'o/_/zas gtsang 
[\[122b.2\]]la bu gnyis te/_/bcom ldan 'das dang /_tshe dang ldan pa dga' bo'o/_/zas dkar la bu gnyis te/_tshe dang ldan pa rgyal dang /_shAkya'i rgyal po bzang ldan no/_/bre bo zas la bu gnyis te/_ming chen dang /_tshe dang ldan pa ma 'gags pa'o/_/bdud rtsi zas 
[\[122b.3\]]kyi bu gnyis te/_tshe dang ldan pa kun dga' bo dang /_lha sbyin no/_/gtsang ma'i bu ni legs par rab sad do/_/dkar mo'i bu ni phreng ba can no/_/bre bo ma'i bu ni bzang len no/_/bdud rtsi ma'i bu ni dge 'phel lo/_/bcom ldan 'das kyi sras ni sgra gcan 'dzin 
[\[122b.4\]]yin te/_gau ta ma dag mang pos bkur gyi rgyud/_tshe dang ldan pa sgra gcan 'dzin du rdzogs/_srid pa'i breng rgyud chad/_'khor ba zad de da ni yang srid pa med do/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis tshe dang ldan pa maud gal gyi bu chen po'i gtam rdzogs par mkhyen 
[\[122b.5\]]nas bzhengs te bzhugs so/_/bzhugs nas tshe dang ldan pa maud gal gyi bu chen po la bka' stsal pa/_maud gal gyi bu legs so legs so/_/maud gal gyi bu khyod kyis dge slong rnams kyi mdun du shAkya rnams kyi sngon gyi rigs rgyud las brtsams nas chos kyi gtam smras 
[\[122b.6\]]pa yang khyod legs so/_/maud gal gyi bu yang khyod kyis shAkya rnams kyi sngon gyi rigs rgyud las brtsams te chos kyi gtam rtag tu smros shig dang /_des ni de rnams la yun ring po'i don dang phan pa dang bde bar gyur to/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis dge 
[\[122b.7\]]slong rnams la bka' stsal pa/_dge slong dag khyed kyis shAkya rnams kyi sngon gyi rigs rgyud las brtsams pa'i chos kyi gtam chongs shig_/zung shig_/lhogs shig_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_dge slong dag shAkya rnams kyi sngon gyi rigs rgyud las brtsams pa'i chos 
[\[123a\]]
[\[123a.1\]]kyi gtam ni don dang ldan chos dang ldan pa nas tshangs par spyod pa'i bar dang ldan pa yin te/_dge slong dag rigs kyi bu dad pas rab tu byung bas shAkya rnams kyi sngon gyi rigs rgyud las brtsams pa'i chos kyi gtam gzung zhing bklag pa'i rigs so/_/
[\[123a.2\]]de nas ser skya'i gzhi'i shAkya rnams bcom ldan 'das kyis gsungs pa la mngon par dga' ste/_rjes su yi rangs nas bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs gnyis la mgo bos phyag byas te bcom ldan 'das kyi spyan snga nas dong ngo /_/de'i tshe grong khyer ser skya'i gzhi na rgyal po seng 
[\[123a.3\]]ge 'gram zhes bya ba rgyal srid byed du 'jug ste/_'byor pa dang /_rgyas pa dang /_bde ba dang /_lo legs pa dang /_skye bo dang /_mi mang pos gang ba/_'thab pa dang /_thab mo dang /_rtsod pa dang /_'gyed pa dang /_chom rkun med cing nad rab tu zhi bar gyur pa/_'bras dang /_bu 
[\[123a.4\]]ram shing gi phreng dang /_ba lang dang /_ma he phun sum tshogs pa na bu gcig pa la sdug pa bzhin du rgyal srid skyong bar byed do/_/grong khyer lhas bstan du rgyal po legs par rab sad ces bya ba rgyal srid byed du 'jug ste/_'byor pa dang /_rgyas pa dang /_bde ba dang /_lo 
[\[123a.5\]]legs pa dang /_skye bo dang mi mang pos gang ba/_'thab pa dang /_thab mo dang /_rtsod pa dang /_'gyed pa dang /_chom rkun med cing nad rab tu zhi bar gyur pa/_'bras dang /_bu ram shing gi phreng ba dang /_ba lang dang /_ma he phun sum tshogs pa na/_bu gcig pa la 
[\[123a.6\]]sdug pa bzhin du rgyal srid skyong bar byed do/_/rgyal po legs par rab sad kyi btsun mo dam pa gzugs bzang ba/_blta na sdug pa/_mdzes pa/_yul gyi bzang mo lum bi nI zhes bya ba yod do/_/lhas bstan na khyim bdag phyug cing nor mang la longs spyod che ba yongs 
[\[123a.7\]]su bzung ba yangs shing rgya che ba/_rnam thos kyi bu'i nor dang ldan pa/_rnam thos kyi bu la nor gyis 'gran pa zhig gnas pa de'i kun dga' ra ba me tog phun sum tshogs pa dang /_'bras bu phun sum tshogs pa dang /_chu phun sum tshogs pa dang /_bya sna tshogs skad 
[\[123b\]]
[\[123b.1\]]'byin pas de'i kun dga' ra ba dga' dga' ltar 'dug pa der rgyal po dus dus su btsun mo'i 'khor dang lhan cig lhags nas de na dga' ba'i rtsed mo nyams su myong bar byed do/_/lum bi nis de mthong nas 'dod pa skyes te/_/des smras pa/_lha kun dga' ra ba 'di 
[\[123b.2\]]bdag la stsal du gsol/_rgyal pos smras pa/_da kun dga' ra ba 'di khyim bdag gi yin na ji ltar sbyin/_gal te khyod la kun dga' ra ba dgos na khyod kyi phyir de bas lhag par bzang ba gzhan zhig bya'o/_/de nas rgyal po legs par rab sad kyis lum bi nI'i 
[\[123b.3\]]ched du de bas khyad par du 'phags pa'i kun dga' ra ba zhig byed du bcug nas/_de'i ming lum bi ni'i tshal zhes bya bar gyur to/_/rgyal po seng ge 'gram la yun ring po nas kye ma'o bdag gi rgyud la 'khor los sgyur ba skye bar gyur kyang ci ma rung snyam pa'i bsam pa 'di yod 
[\[123b.4\]]do/_/rgyal po legs par rab sad la yang yun ring po nas kye ma'o bdag ni seng ge 'gram dang lhan cig gnyen du gyur kyang ci ma rung snyam pa'i bsam pa 'di yod do/_/ji tsam du dus gzhan zhig na de'i btsun mo dang lhan cig rtse bar byed/_dga' bar byed/_dga' dgur 
[\[123b.5\]]spyod pa las dus gzhan zhig na btsun mo sems can dang ldan par gyur nas/_de zla ba brgyad dam dgu 'das pa na bu mo gzugs bzang ba/_blta na sdug pa/_mdzes pa/_yan lag dang /_nying lag thams cad dang ldan pa zhig btsas so/_/btsas pa de'i gzugs bzang 
[\[123b.6\]]pos rgyal po legs par rab sad btsun mo'i 'khor dang bcas pa dang lhas bstan na gnas pa'i skye bo'i tshogs rnams mchog tu ngo mtshar skyes te/_the tsom du gyur nas ci 'di bu mo yin nam/_'on te las sna tshogs pas sprul pa'i sgyu 'phrul yin snyam mo/_/de'i btsas pa'i 
[\[123b.7\]]btsas ston nyi ma bdun gsum nyi shu gcig tu rgya cher byas nas/_bu mo 'di'i ming cir gdags zhes ming 'dogs par byed do/_/blon po rnams kyis smras pa/_lhas bstan na gnas pa'i skye bo'i tshogs lam po che dang /_tshong 'dus dang /_sum mdo dang /_bzhi mdo 
[\[124a\]]
[\[124a.1\]]rnams su gleng ba kha cig gi mchid nas ni 'di bu mo kho na lags te/_sngon gyi las kyi rnam par smin pas gzugs bzang zhing kha dog phun sum tshogs par gyur to zhes mchi'o/_/gzhan dag gi mchid nas ni 'di bu mo ma lags kyi 'o na ci zhe na las sna 
[\[124a.2\]]tshogs pas sprul pa'i sgyu 'phrul lags so zhes mchi lags pas/_de bas na bu mo'i ming sgyu 'phrul zhes bgyi bar gdags so zhes btags so/_/bu mo sgyu 'phrul ma ma brgyad la rjes su gtad de zhes bya ba nas chen mor gyur to zhes bya ba'i bar gong ma bzhin du'o/_/de la mtshan 
[\[124a.3\]]mkhan rnams kyis lung bstan pa/_mtshan phun sum tshogs pa du ma dang ldan pa zhig btsa' bar 'gyur zhing stobs kyis 'khor los sgyur bar 'gyur ro zhes bstan to/_/yang de rtse bar byed/_dga' bar byed/_dga' dgur spyod par byed pa las bu mo gzugs bzang ba/_blta 
[\[124a.4\]]na sdug pa mdzes pa/_kha dog bzang mo rgyas pa mchog dang ldan pa zhig btsas so/_/de btsas pa na grong khyer de dag thams cad snang ba rgya chen pos snang bar byas te/_sgyu 'phrul la ji lta ba bzhin du de'i gzugs bzang ba rnam pa thams cad du dpyad par mi nus so/_/de'i btsas 
[\[124a.5\]]pa'i btsas ston yang rgya cher byas nas/_bu mo 'di'i ming cir gdags zhes ming 'dogs par byed do/_/blon po rnams kyis smras pa/_sgyu 'phrul bas 'di lhag par gzugs bzang bas/_sgyu 'phrul chen mo zhes bgyi bar gdags so zhes btags so/_/de yang bskyed 
[\[124a.6\]]bsrings nas chen mor gyur pa dang /_de la mtshan mkhan rnams kyis lung bstan pa/_skyes bu chen po'i mtshan sum cu rtsa gnyis dang ldan pa zhig btsa' bar 'gyur te/_de ni 'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal por 'gyur ro zhes lung bstan to/_/rgyal po legs par rab 
[\[124a.7\]]sad kyis rgyal po seng ge 'gram la pho nya btang ste/_bdag la bu mo gnyis shig btsas pa ni sgyu 'phrul dang /_sgyu 'phrul chen mo lags te/_de la gcig la ni mtshan thams cad phun sum tshogs pa'i bu zhig btsa' bar 'gyur zhing /_de ni stobs 
[\[124b\]]
[\[124b.1\]]kyis 'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal por 'gyur ro zhes lung bstan to/_/cig shos la ni skyes bu chen po'i mtshan sum cu rtsa gnyis kyis brgyan pa'i bu zhig btsa' bar 'gyur zhing /_de ni 'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal por 'gyur ro zhes lung bstan gyis 'di gnyis las 
[\[124b.2\]]gang bzhed pa/_gzhon nu zas gtsang gi chung mar snoms shig_/seng ge 'gram gyis slar spring pa/_gnyis ka yang gzhon nu la dgos mod kyi/_'on kyang chung ma 'dra ba gnyis gcig la gzhag par mi bya bas/_gang la skyes bu chen po'i 
[\[124b.3\]]mtshan sum cu rtsa gnyis kyis brgyan pa'i bu btsa' bar 'gyur zhing /_de yang 'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal por 'gyur ro zhes lung bstan pa de re zhig 'dod do/_/cig shos kyang tshogs la brtag par bya'o/_/des de 'khor lnga brgya dang bkri'o/_/
[\[124b.4\]]de'i tshe shAkya rnams kyi phyir rgol ba skya bseng zhes bya ba ri khrod pa cig yod nas shAkya rnams 'dus te/_rgyal po seng ge 'gram gyi drung du song nas/_lha skya ba seng ri khrod pa bdag cag la gnod pa bgyid kyis grogs mdzad du gsol/_des smras pa/_shes 
[\[124b.5\]]ldan dag kho bo rgas pas de rnams dang lhan cig g.yul sprad par mi nus so/_/lha gzhon nu zas gtsang gtang du gsol/_dam zhig bca' ste/_gal te gzhon nu ji lta bu 'dod pa'i dam pa gcig bslangs pa'i dam pa de sbyin pa lta na gtang ngo /_/de 
[\[124b.6\]]rnams kyis smras pa/_lha bka' bzhin 'tshal stsal bar bgyi'o/_/rgyal pos dpung gi tshogs yan lag bzhi pa gzhon nu zas gtsang la bsngo nas des ri khrod pa de rnams bcom/_rab tu bcom ste rmeg btang ngo /_/de nas shAkya rnams mgu nas rgyal 
[\[124b.7\]]po seng ge 'gram gyi drung du dong ste lha gzhon nus ri khrod pa rnams bcom rab tu bcom ste rmeg btang lags pas bdag cag glo ba dga' na/_gzhon nu mchog ci bzhed gsungs shig dbul lo/_/shes ldan dag shAkya rnams kyi khrims su bcas pa sus 
[\[125a\]]
[\[125a.1\]]kyang mnyam pa las chun ma gzhag par mi bya'o zhes bcas so/_/lha ci khrims 'dral lam/_des smras pa/_lhag par shin tu bsdam gyi dral bar mi bya na/_'on kyang gzhon nu'i dam pa gcig slong ba ni mnyam pa las chun ma blang ba yin no/_/
[\[125a.2\]]lha rung gis de bzhin du mdzod cig_/de nas seng ge 'gram gyis legs par rab sad la pho nya btang nas/_kho bos tshogs brtags zin gyis bu mo cig shos kyang bkri shig_/des de yang 'khor lnga brgya dang bkri ste/_gzhon nu zas gtsang gis gnyi ga blangs so/_/
[\[125a.3\]]ji tsam dus gzhan zhig na rgyal po seng ge 'gram shi nas/_ser skya'i gzhi na rgyal po zas gtsang rgyal srid byed du bcug ste/_'byor pa dang /_rgyas pa dang /_bde ba dang /_lo legs pa dang /_skye bo dang /_mi mang pos gang ba/_'thab pa dang /_thab mo dang /_

[\[125a.6\]]par mdzad do/_/rigs la gzigs pa dang /_yul la gzigs pa dang /_dus la gzigs pa dang /_rgyud la gzigs pa dang /_bud med la gzigs pa ste/_rgya cher mdo snga ma dang sbyar ro/_/mngon par 'byung ba'i mdo rdzogs so//_//rgya gar gyi mkhan po
